### method jfzero(dt=SINGLE, freg1=xmm0, freg2=xmm1, greg=r8, label=someloop)

### method jvzero(dt=DOUBLE, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=xmm0, vreg2=xmm1)

vcmpeqpd %xmm1,%xmm0,%xmm1

### method jvzero(dt=HALF, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=xmm0, vreg2=xmm1)

### method jvzero(dt=SINGLE, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=xmm0, vreg2=xmm1)

vptest %xmm1,%xmm1
jne .someloop
